ODIS regional and town buses · Českotěšínsko · Frýdecko-Místecko · Frýdlantsko · Jablunkovsko–Třinecko · MHD Český Těšín · Frýdek-Místek · Třinec · vypravenost · Tuesday 20 Oct 2026

ODIS Těšínsko Třinecko Frýdecko

The ODIS bus lines of four regional contract areas (Českotěšínsko, Frýdecko-Místecko, Frýdlantsko and Jablunkovsko–Třinecko, Transdev Slezsko) and of the town networks of Český Těšín and Třinec (ARRIVA autobusy) and Frýdek-Místek (Transdev Slezsko), set as Simutrans lines with the owner's regional method: each line read over the whole day at its core points, a repeating interval, destination and shift variants on their own longer intervals (at most 480 min), timetables at the terminals worked back from the knots, every afternoon stop on a line, buses sized from the pattern. Buses are the ones seen on each line on the ODIS live map on Tuesday 20 Oct 2026.

Network state, 20 Oct 2026

What is in the model and what is left out, with the reason.

modelledFour regional contract areas and three town networks

Českotěšínsko (licences 871xxx), Frýdecko-Místecko (863xxx), Frýdlantsko (864xxx, added 21 Oct) and Jablunkovsko–Třinecko (861xxx, one area) of Transdev Slezsko; the town lines of Frýdek-Místek (865xxx, Transdev Slezsko), Český Těšín (875xxx) and Třinec (866xxx), both ARRIVA autobusy. Each has its own section below.

modelledDiversion timetables to the end of the timetable year

The CIS JŘ export has diversion versions valid on the vehicle day until 12 Dec 2026 for 770, 786, 787 (Třinec – Bystřice – Jablunkov), 315 and 316 (Palkovice: 315 skips Adámek, Podhůří and u Kožuchů), 704 and 716 (Třinec town lines; 716 runs 21 trips instead of 7, via Guty, škola and Kanada) and 721 and 722 (Český Těšín, via Nová Tovární, Walmark, točna and Tovární u trati). They run until the end of the timetable year, so they are modelled as they run.

left outLine 731: Stonava mines diversion (28 Aug – 30 Nov)

The diversion skips Stonava, Důl ČSM-Jih, Důl ČSM-Sever and Bonkov rozcestí. It ends in six weeks, so the regular route is modelled (shared rule: short works as the regular state), as for 582 on the Havířovsko/Karvinsko/Orlovsko sheet.

left out383, 387, 365 and 655

383 and 387 (Frýdecko-Místecko licences) stop at Ostrava, Hranečník and are on the Ostrava sheet (section All lines at Hranečník). 365 Havířov – Frýdek-Místek is on the Havířovsko/Karvinsko/Orlovsko regional sheet. 655 Frenštát – Trojanovice is on the Novojičínsko/Bílovecko sheet, where its main licence (883655, 41 of 45 trips) is. Their buses stay there.

Each bus shows its fleet number and branch, full type and length (with its number plate), and pak128 core with livery. Green border: seen running this line on the live map that afternoon (the line's window first; the trip is named). Dashed border: designed, not seen in the window, with the reason. Dimmed, dotted: seen, not needed on this line.

How to read the line cards

Typing the timetable. Values go into the Scheduling tab of the terminal entry, with the calendar on: Departure every, Offset, Also at (at most 7). Offsets count from 01:00 and every terminal is set as a loop, so each value is (real departure − 61) mod interval; the real clock minute is under every value. A row with Every 0 has no timetable: the bus leaves on arrival.

Core points and knots. Each line was read over the whole operating day (5:00–23:00) at its core points: terminals, knots where variants split, shared hubs. A trip counts at the time it passes a core point, wherever it starts. Where a slot is defined at a knot, the terminal departure is the knot time minus the running time, and the knot gets no timetable.

Variants. Each destination, path or shift variant is its own Simutrans line on a longer interval (the save game's Ex4). Shift lines repeat every 480 (8 h), the longest interval allowed. Trips more than 3 min off every slot of the line are off the pattern and shown in orange.

Buses needed. One bus followed round the line's own slots with the real running times and at least 3 min at each terminal (less where the slots chain closer, as the real timetable turns). Real buses swap lines at Třinec, Český Těšín, Jablunkov, Frýdek-Místek and Frýdlant all afternoon, so per-line totals are higher than what Transdev runs; pooling lines would be the owner's choice and is not done.

To check

First, five choices made here that you may want to flip, each with its alternative. Then the Simutrans lines whose model window has a slot without a real departure, or a real departure more than 3 min off: mostly the real timetable is irregular there, and the card says why.

Irisbus Crossway LE 12M

  • 401 · line 347Crossway_LE_City_12M · 3csadbilozelena

Pak128 objects

Pak session f3, 20 Oct 2026: regional buses take VZ-ODIS-bus; the town buses of Třinec, Český Těšín and Frýdek-Místek take their town packages. A regional bus (kraj logo) lent to a town line keeps its VZ-ODIS-bus object. Transdev Slezsko's 3ČSAD white-green (with or without the kraj logo) is 3csadbilozelena, the red-black ODIS scheme odiscervenocerna. Objects are read from the installed pak (VZ-ODIS-bus, VZ-MHDTrinec-bus, VZ-MHDCeskyTesin-bus, VZ-MHDFrydekMistek-bus); “≈” marks a stand-in, listed in pak_request.md. Length is in car units (16 = one tile).

How this was built

Scope. Licences with the area prefixes 871 (Českotěšínsko), 863 (Frýdecko-Místecko), 864 (Frýdlantsko), 861 (Jablunkovsko–Třinecko) of Transdev Slezsko, 865 (MHD Frýdek-Místek) of Transdev Slezsko and 875 (MHD Český Těšín) and 866 (MHD Třinec) of ARRIVA autobusy in the CIS JŘ bus export (CIS JŘ bus JDF export of 20 Oct 2026 (stored by the Havířov/Karviná/Orlová pipeline)), evaluated for Tuesday 20 Oct 2026 with the shared version rule. The prefixes match the live map (provozovna Český Těšín, Frýdek-Místek (which runs Frýdecko-Místecko and Frýdlantsko), Jablunkov; ARRIVA Třinec) and the kraj’s list of contract areas. 101 ODIS lines; 79 are modelled here, 4 are on other sheets, the rest are listed with the reason.

Method. pipeline/bus-schedule-method.md (v3, the owner's regional rules of 20 Oct 2026): whole-day reading at core points, repeating interval, Ex4 variants, timing at terminals worked back from knots, no daily lines (at most every 480), every afternoon stop on a line, buses from the pattern, every line checked by hand. Each line's choices are in config.json → lines and in the README.

Buses. The ODIS live map (mpvnet.cz) polled every minute 13:05–19:30 on Tuesday 20 Oct 2026 by the Havířov/Karviná/Orlová pipeline gives every bus's trips (licence and trip number), joined to fleet numbers by number plate (seznam-autobusu.cz register). A bus fills one Simutrans line it ran in that line's window, by matching. Buses already on another sheet (Ostrava Bus Vypravenost, section All lines at Hranečník; Havířov Karviná Orlová Bus Vypravenost (town lines); ODIS Regional Havířovsko Karvinsko Orlovsko; ODIS Novojičínsko Bílovecko) are not used here. ARRIVA’s town buses and Z-Group’s buses on 980 and 985 are not in the Transdev register: their plates were looked up on seznam-autobusu.cz vehicle pages (01b_plates.py). The two Český Těšín buses with vanity plates, TESIN 001 and TESIN 004, are in the register as “001 TESIN” and “004 TESIN”: Scania Citywide LF 10,9M of 2018 in the town’s white-yellow livery.

Sources: CIS JŘ bus JDF, ODIS live map, Transdev Slezsko register, seznam-autobusu.cz vehicle pages (ARRIVA).
